# Semantic renames applied to the decompiled sources.
#
#   C <class>                          <newName>
#   F <class> <field>                  <newName>
#   M <class> <name(types)>            <newName>
#   V <class> <name(types)> <variable> <newName>
#
# Types in a key are the simple names the source spells (arrays as int[]).
# Applied by tools/Rename.java (javac resolves every occurrence), in two phases:
# declarations first (`decl`), then variables (`vars`). Chained entries are
# replayed until they stop changing anything, which is what
# tools/merge-renames.sh does. The file is the history of the pass, so early
# entries stay even when a later entry supersedes them.

# ------------------------------------------------------------ J2ME shims
# The desktop shims under javax/microedition and com/ea/sims3/m3g were written
# by hand for this port and their identifiers were tidied directly (they are
# not decompiler output, so they never went through tools/Rename.java):
#   g -> awtGraphics / graphics, img -> image, in -> stream, fm -> fontMetrics,
#   w/h -> rectWidth/rectHeight, dw/dh -> destWidth/destHeight,
#   ch -> character, vk -> keyCode, bg -> gameBuffer, lg -> logicalGraphics,
#   a -> alpha, r -> red, b -> blue, l -> playerListener, e -> event/failure,
#   d -> current / currentDisplay, f/n/i -> storeFile/storeName/index.
